# Helper removal gates

Helpers are retained until every listed consumer has moved or its duplicate disposition has been implemented. Structural Oracle/registry checks and executable fixtures have different lifetimes. No helper is removed by #400.

## Shared Cirdan module

[`cirdanCreatureCards.ts`](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanCreatureCards.ts) reads the stored Cirdan deck for `deckCard`/`deckOracle`, creates generic creature/artifact/library fixtures, and wraps combat/entry/selection operations. Its consumers are:

- [rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanGrothamaGuardian.test.ts](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanGrothamaGuardian.test.ts): allNamed, artifact, choose, deckCard, enter, fixtureCreature, libraries, libraryOf, named.
- [rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanEldrazi.test.ts](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanEldrazi.test.ts): deckCard, fixtureCreature, combatGame, named.
- [rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanStatics.test.ts](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanStatics.test.ts): allNamed, deckCard, fixtureCreature, named.
- [rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanColossi.test.ts](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanColossi.test.ts): allNamed, deckCard, fixtureCreature, named.
- [rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanTriggers.test.ts](https://github.com/lordnox/commander-decks/blob/486f73779424dc94fdaaa2712337462994ebe2e6/rules-engine/src/cardPlugins/cirdanTriggers.test.ts): allNamed, artifact, choose, deckCard, enter, fixtureCreature, libraries, named.

**Gate:** #401 preserves exact printed characteristics/registration where needed; #402/#403 replace the deck dependency with exchangeable mechanic fixtures or catalog-backed integration fixtures. Preserve combat step advancement, owner/controller distinctions, and typed selections before deleting `combatGame`, `enter`, or `choose`. Remove the module only after a repository-wide import search finds no remaining consumers.

## Status inventories and loader wrappers

The PART36/PART37 assignment tables, GAP_REASON maps, `knownPlugins`, local `real`/`oracleFor`/`fromPool` loaders, kernel handle adapters, and pass/advance wrappers are not shared gameplay semantics by themselves. #401 should reconcile supported/unsupported status with the current registry and coverage ledger; #402/#403 keep the assertion and runtime-loading behavior required by their consumers. Do not replace a supported-card assertion with a stale historical GAP note. `plugDackPart37` exports its assignment/status constants; perform a repository-wide consumer search before removing those exports. The audit found no cross-file consumers of those exported constants in the inspected source tree.
